## Mission

Pixie's Pantry operates the first Clinical Hardware Registry for thermal extraction devices and botanical accessories. Every product is evaluated under our proprietary MGV-1 (Medical-Grade Verification) framework before being listed. We exist to bring pharmaceutical-grade accountability to an industry that has operated without verifiable standards. Our registry ensures that every device, accessory, and component meets documented criteria for material safety, thermal precision, and construction integrity.

## MGV-1 Clinical Framework

The MGV-1 (Medical-Grade Verification Level 1) framework is our proprietary audit standard. It evaluates hardware across three core dimensions:

### Precision Thermal Control (PTC)
- Measures temperature accuracy within ±2°F across the full operating range
- Verifies consistent heat distribution across the material chamber
- Documents heat-up time, session stability, and thermal recovery between draws
- Ensures operation below the combustion threshold of 455°F (235°C)

### Thermal Transfer Integrity (TTI)
- Requires ≥98% inert vapor path (no off-gassing from internal components)
- Tests all materials in the airpath for chemical stability at operating temperatures
- Verifies no degradation of seals, gaskets, or chamber linings during extended use

### Surgical-Grade Construction (SGC)
- Materials tested against ISO 10993 (biological evaluation of medical devices)
- Glass components verified to USP Class VI standards
- Metals evaluated for heavy-metal leaching under thermal stress
- Zero-shedding requirement for grinder mechanisms

## Clinical Lexicon

Pixie's Pantry uses a clinical terminology system to accurately describe product categories:

- **Thermal Extraction Device (TED)**: Consumer term: vaporizer/vape. A device that heats botanical material below combustion temperature to release active compounds as an aerosol.
- **Digital Desktop Nebulizer**: Consumer term: desktop vape. A stationary TED with precision digital controls, typically with higher thermal mass and clinical-grade temperature regulation.
- **Aerosol Delivery System**: Consumer term: portable vape. A battery-powered handheld TED designed for on-demand thermal extraction.
- **Hydrated Particulate Filter**: Consumer term: water pipe. A glass filtration system that passes aerosol through water to cool and filter particulate matter.
- **Botanical Homogenizer**: Consumer term: grinder. A zero-shedding mechanical device that reduces botanical material to uniform particle size for optimal thermal extraction.
- **Concentrate Administration Console**: Consumer term: dab rig/concentrate rig. A specialized device designed for controlled vaporization of botanical concentrates.
- **Pressure Regulation Valve**: Consumer term: carb cap. A component that regulates airflow and chamber pressure during concentrate administration.
- **Variable Power Supply Unit (VPSU)**: Consumer term: battery/mod. An electronic power delivery system with adjustable voltage or wattage output.
- **Internal Dispersion Tube**: Consumer term: downstem. A submerged tube that disperses aerosol into the filtration medium.
- **Clinical Extract Applicator**: Consumer term: dab tool. A precision instrument for measuring and applying concentrate doses.
- **Atmospherically-Stabilized Storage Vessel**: Consumer term: storage jar. An airtight container engineered to maintain optimal humidity and freshness.
- **Borosilicate Glass Respiratory Aid**: Consumer term: pipe. A borosilicate glass device for direct thermal extraction without water filtration.
- **Chemical Sterilization Agent**: Consumer term: cleaning supplies. Specialized solutions for maintaining sterile airpath and chamber conditions.
- **Material Receptacle**: Consumer term: bowl. The chamber component that holds botanical material during thermal extraction.

### Prism

- Headquarters: United States
- Founded: 2020
- Products in registry: 196
- Price range: $9.99–$136.99
- Positioning: Budget ($3–$65). Essential components and upgrades.

Comprehensive parts and accessories catalog covering replacement glass, downstems, bowls, adapters, ash catchers, and modular components. Provides the connective tissue for building and maintaining custom hydro-filtration setups.

Key products: Extensive range of glass adapters (14mm, 18mm, 10mm), replacement downstems (various lengths), bowl pieces, ash catchers, drop-down adapters, reclaim catchers, bangers (quartz nails), carb caps, and terp pearls.

Materials: Borosilicate glass, quartz, food-grade silicone, stainless steel

- Headquarters: United States

- Official site: N/A (exclusive to authorized retailers)
- Products in registry: 118
- Price range: $2.99–$84.99
- Positioning: Accessible ($3–$85). Broadest selection in registry.

Borosilicate glass hydro-filtration systems, water pipes, recyclers, rigs, and botanical accessories. Largest product line in the registry covering everything from mini rigs to large straight tubes, ash catchers, bowls, downstems, and grinders.

Key products: Extensive catalog of glass water pipes in multiple form factors (beaker, straight tube, recycler, Swiss rig, gumball rig), ash catchers (dry, recycler type), replacement bowls and downstems, botanical homogenizers (grinders), rolling trays, and storage solutions.

Materials: Borosilicate glass, food-grade silicone, stainless steel, aluminum

### Puffco

- Headquarters: Los Angeles, California, USA
- Founded: 2013
- Official site: puffco.com
- Products in registry: 64
- Price range: $18.99–$614.99
- Positioning: Premium ($19–$615). Market leader in concentrate devices.

Concentrate-focused thermal extraction devices and accessories. Pioneer of the electronic concentrate administration console category. Known for the Peak series (smart dab rigs) and the Proxy modular system.

Key products: Peak Pro (smart concentrate device with real-time temperature control and Bluetooth app), Peak (original electronic concentrate rig), Proxy (modular handheld system with interchangeable glass), New Peak Pro (latest generation with 3DXL chamber), Cupsy (discreet portable), Hot Knife (heated loading tool).

Materials: Ceramic heating elements, borosilicate glass attachments, anodized aluminum, medical-grade silicone

### Jane West

- Headquarters: Denver, Colorado, USA
- Founded: 2015
- Products in registry: 60
- Price range: $18.99–$242.99
- Positioning: Mid-range. Lifestyle-driven design.

Luxury lifestyle accessories and glass designed for the modern consumer. Fashion-forward pieces that challenge outdated stigma with clean lines, muted colors, and sophisticated silhouettes. Pioneer of the cannabis lifestyle brand concept.

Key products: Beakers, bubblers, one-hitters, spoons, chillums, and steamrollers. Signature aesthetic features frosted glass, minimal branding, and earth-tone color palettes. Designed by Jane West herself.

Materials: Premium borosilicate glass, frosted finishes, silicone accents

### Storz & Bickel

- Headquarters: Tuttlingen, Germany
- Founded: 2000
- Official site: storz-bickel.com
- Products in registry: 45
- Price range: $6.99–$920.99
- Positioning: Premium ($7–$921). Industry benchmark for thermal precision.

Precision desktop and portable thermal extraction devices. Manufacturer of the Volcano (the world's first commercial convection vaporizer) and the Mighty/Crafty portable series. Medical-device certified in Germany and Canada. ISO 13485 certified manufacturing.

Key products: Volcano Hybrid (desktop convection/conduction hybrid), Mighty+ (portable with USB-C rapid charging), Crafty+ (compact Bluetooth-enabled portable), Plenty (high-throughput handheld). All devices feature precision temperature control within ±2°F and use food-safe/medical-grade materials in the airpath.

Materials: Surgical-grade stainless steel, food-safe PEEK thermoplastic, borosilicate glass, medical-grade silicone seals
